The lower bound runs through a \(p\)-nuclear obstruction: an aggregate column-width estimate \(D_k(T_n)\asymp n^{3/2}k^{-1/2}\), valid in the low-rank range \(1\leq k\leq n/16\), for the prefix chain, fed into the classical approximation-space conversion of Pietsch and Hinrichs--Pietsch, becomes harmonic at the critical exponent \(p=2/3\), and Hölder's inequality transfers it to both factorization costs. The same computation determines \(\nucpow_p(T_n)\) for each fixed \(0<p<1\): order \(n\) below \(2/3\), \(n\log n\) at \(2/3\), and \(n^{3p/2}\) above. A Fenwick interval factorization supplies matching upper bounds. The claims are confined to pure-\(\eps\)-DP Laplace matrix mechanisms and the two stated squared-error criteria; they do not cover non-matrix continual mechanisms, approximate-DP sensitivity, or expected maxima across coordinates.

</details>


### [122] [Blockchain Transaction Simulation Phishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28747)
*Xiaocan Wang,Shixuan Guan,Tong Yang,Xiapu Luo,Yue Duan,Kai Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了"交易模拟钓鱼"（transaction simulation phishing）攻击——攻击者利用依赖动态区块链状态的精心构造的智能合约，使钱包的交易模拟显示正常或盈利结果，而实际上链执行时会将用户资金转移到攻击者控制的地址。作者提出了SIMGUARD（基于字节码的检测系统），在以太坊等四条链上检测出超过4,000个钓鱼合约，涉及5,700多名受害者和约348万美元损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流加密钱包（如MetaMask）通过交易模拟功能预览交易的余额变化来防御传统钓鱼攻击，但这种防御机制本身可以被新型钓鱼技术利用。交易模拟系统通常假设模拟结果与实际执行一致，而攻击者可以利用动态区块链状态使模拟与实际执行产生差异，从而绕过现有防御。此前尚无针对这一新型攻击模式的系统性研究。

Method: 首先开发了可用于促进此类攻击的钓鱼合约分类体系（taxonomy）；随后提出了SIMGUARD——一个基于字节码级别的检测系统，结合静态与动态程序分析来识别钓鱼合约；最后在多条区块链（以太坊、币安智能链、Avalanche、Polygon）上进行大规模应用验证，并对检测到的钓鱼合约进行聚类分析。

Result: 在2024年8月至2025年6月期间，共检测到超过4,000个钓鱼合约，识别出5,700多名受害者，总损失约348万美元，其中91.5%发生在以太坊上。聚类结果显示最大的钓鱼合约集群单独就占了总损失的约83%。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了当前钱包防御机制中的关键弱点，凸显了开发更健壮的交易模拟机制以应对此类新型钓鱼攻击的紧迫性。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency users have increasingly become targets of phishing and scam attacks. To mitigate these threats, leading crypto wallets (e.g., MetaMask) have introduced transaction simulation, which previews a transaction's balance changes before on-chain execution. While effective against traditional fund-draining attacks, we show that this defense can itself be exploited by a new phishing technique, which we term transaction simulation phishing. This attack uses carefully crafted smart contracts whose execution depends on dynamic blockchain state, causing simulations to display benign or profitable outcomes while the actual on-chain execution redirects users' funds to attacker-controlled addresses.
  We present the first comprehensive study of transaction simulation phishing. We first develop a taxonomy of phishing contracts that can be utilized to facilitate this attack. Then, we propose SIMGUARD, a bytecode-level detection system that combines static and dynamic program analysis to identify phishing contracts. Applying SIMGUARD to Ethereum, Binance Smart Chain, Avalanche, and Polygon, we detect over 4,000 phishing contracts deployed between August 2024 and June 2025. Our analysis identifies more than 5,700 victims and approximately $3.48 million USD in losses, 91.5% of which occurred on Ethereum. Moreover, our clustering result reveals that the largest phishing contract cluster alone accounts for about 83% of the total losses. These results expose a critical weakness in current wallet defenses and highlight the urgent need for more robust transaction simulation mechanisms.

</details>


### [123] [Partial Derandomization for Leakage-Resilient Shamir's Secret Sharing over Composite Order Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28757)
*S. Venkitesh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对复合阶域上抗泄漏Shamir秘密共享的显式求值位置构造问题取得进展，通过对求值位置进行部分去随机化，将随机性从nd log p比特降至d log p比特，并在特定参数范围内实现完全安全（统计距离为零）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机求值位置虽能保证复合阶域上Shamir秘密共享的统计安全性（Maji等人，EUROCRYPT 2024），但随机性消耗过高（nd log p比特）。Nguyen（EUROCRYPT 2025）建立了一个二分法，指出线性码秘密共享方案要么统计安全要么完全不安全。基于此二分法，本文希望减少随机性开销，探索更高效的求值位置构造方式。

Method: 基于Nguyen的二分法，将n个独立随机求值位置替换为简单固定有理函数Φ的迭代序列x_j = Φ^j(x_0)，其中初始点x_0从F_{p^d}^*中随机选取。利用部分分式非退化论证，利用有理迭代的分母具有不同极点这一性质来证明安全性。该构造适用于n = O(d/log_p d)的参数范围以及任意重建阈值k ≥ 2。

Result: 求值位置的随机性开销从nd log p比特降至d log p比特，实现了部分去随机化。在该构造下，方案对单块泄漏达到完美安全（统计距离精确为零），适用于n = O(d/log_p d)且k ≥ 2的参数范围。

Conclusion: 本文在Maji等人和Nguyen工作的基础上，通过有理函数迭代实现了求值位置的部分去随机化，显著降低了随机性消耗，并在特定参数范围内实现了完美的单块泄漏安全性，为复合阶域上抗泄漏秘密共享的实际构造提供了更高效的途径。

Abstract: We make progress on the question of constructing explicit evaluation places for leakage-resilient Shamir's secret sharing, over composite order fields. Previously, Maji et al. (EUROCRYPT 2024) showed that random evaluation places yield Shamir's secret sharing over the composite order field $\mathbb{F}_{p^d}$ that is statistically secure against physical-bit leakage. Later, Nguyen (EUROCRYPT 2025) established a dichotomy that linear code-based secret-sharing scheme over the field $\mathbb{F}_{p^d}$ is either statistically secure or completely insecure against such leakage.
  Building upon Nguyen's dichotomy, we present a partial derandomization of evaluation places, improving upon the Maji et al. result for a restricted regime of parameters. We replace the random choice of $n$ independent evaluation places by the iterates $x_j = Φ^j(x_0)$ of a simple fixed rational function $Φ$, where the initial point $x_0 \in \mathbb{F}_{p^d}^*$ is randomly chosen. The randomness in the evaluation places thus drops from $nd \log p$ bits to $d\log p$ bits. Our construction is valid for the regime $n = O(d/\log_p d)$, and any reconstruction threshold $k \ge 2$; in fact, the scheme attains perfect security (statistical distance exactly zero) against single-block leakage. Our technique is a partial fraction nondegeneracy argument that exploits the distinct poles of the rational iterates.

</details>


### [124] [YazSes: An Offline, Privacy-First, Cross-Platform Hold-to-Talk Voice-Dictation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28878)
*Mohsen Seyedkazemi Ardebili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: YazSes 是一个完全在设备端运行的、开源（Apache-2.0）的按住即说话语音听写守护进程，基于协议化平台抽象层，单一代码库支持 Linux、macOS 和 Windows。它使用 faster-whisper（CPU, int8）进行本地语音转写，配备快速正则命令语法和小型语言模型路由器，确保任何数据不外泄。在 200 条 LibriSpeech 测试集上，词错误率从 tiny.en 的 4.82% 到 small.en 的 2.59%，实时因子 0.520，命令语法达到 100% 动作准确率、0% 误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云语音听写服务虽然准确率高，但需要将用户语音流式传输到远程服务商，这在隐私敏感职业和离线/隔离环境中不可接受；现有的端侧替代方案要么被平台锁定，要么面向专家脚本而非即插即用的听写场景。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个跨平台、完全本地、隐私安全的语音听写解决方案。

Method: YazSes 采用分层流水线架构，通过协议化平台抽象层实现一套代码库跨三平台运行，使用 JSON-RPC 控制平面。语音通过按住即说话方式采集（非持续监听），由 faster-whisper（CPU、int8 量化）本地转写并注入当前聚焦应用；快速正则表达式命令语法（可选配小型语言模型路由器）将语音映射为编辑器和终端操作。隐私设计包括无遥测、可选个性化循环保持语料库加密在设备本地，仅提出配置变更建议而不导出数据。评估使用可复现基准测试框架。

Result: 在单台商用 Linux 笔记本上，200 条 LibriSpeech test-clean（40 位说话人）上，词错误率范围为 4.82%（tiny.en）至 2.59%（small.en），small.en 的实时因子为 0.520（无 GPU 的 CPU 上快于实时解码）。命令语法在纯听写场景达到 100% 动作准确率和 0.0% 误报率，每次调用耗时 0.021 ms，非解码流水线额外开销仅 0.289 ms。macOS 和 Windows 后端已实现并通过单元测试，但未做端到端评估。

Conclusion: YazSes 证明了在纯 CPU 设备上实现跨平台、高准确率、完全本地化的语音听写与命令执行是可行的，同时满足严格的隐私要求（无数据外泄、无遥测）。系统与可复现基准测试框架均已开源，为隐私敏感环境（如医疗、法律、涉密及离线场景）提供了实用的端侧语音听写替代方案。

Abstract: Cloud voice-dictation services deliver strong accuracy but require streaming a user's speech to a remote provider, an unacceptable trade-off in privacy-sensitive professions and offline or air-gapped settings; the leading on-device alternatives are either platform-locked or aimed at expert scripting rather than plug-and-play dictation. We present YazSes, an open-source (Apache-2.0) hold-to-talk voice dictation daemon that runs entirely on-device, with a single codebase targeting Linux, macOS, and Windows through a protocol-based platform abstraction. YazSes transcribes speech locally with faster-whisper (CPU, int8) and injects the result into the focused application; a fast regex command grammar, backed by an optional small-language-model router, maps utterances to editor and terminal actions. Nothing leaves the machine: recording is push-to-talk rather than always-listening, there is no telemetry, and an opt-in personalization loop keeps its corpus encrypted on-device and proposes configuration changes instead of shipping data out. We describe the system architecture -- a staged pipeline behind a protocol-based platform abstraction with a JSON-RPC control plane -- and its privacy and threat model. We evaluate the shipping Python implementation on a single commodity Linux laptop; the macOS and Windows backends are implemented and unit-tested but not end-to-end evaluated here. On 200 LibriSpeech test-clean utterances spanning 40 speakers, word error rate ranges from 4.82% (tiny.en) to 2.59% (small.en) at a real-time factor of 0.520 for small.en, decoding faster than real time on CPU with no GPU. The command grammar reaches 100% action accuracy with a 0.0% false-positive rate on plain dictation at 0.021 ms per call, and the non-decode pipeline adds 0.289 ms of overhead. The system and the reproducible benchmark harness behind every number in this paper are public.

</details>


### [125] [Hollow-LLM Attack: Computationally Trivial Weights in Zero-Knowledge Verification of LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28884)
*Chen Gong,Beijie Liu,Mengyuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了零知识（ZK）大语言模型推理验证中的一个关键盲区：ZK证明虽然能验证输出与公开架构和私有权重的一致性（等式正确性），但无法绑定实际付出的计算工作量。作者形式化了这一"努力差距"（effort gap），并提出"空心LLM攻击"（Hollow-LLM Attack），使不诚实的服务提供商保留声明架构和参数数量，但嵌入代数结构坍缩的"幽灵权重"（ghost weights），从而以远小于声明模型的计算成本产生有效的ZK证明，实现"正确输出+小模型成本+虚报模型规模"的盈利均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM规模日益增大且主要部署于远程平台，验证推理执行的忠实性至关重要。ZK推理验证虽然承诺公开可验证性和等式正确性，但作者发现其无法约束实际计算付出。这构成了验证体系中的根本性漏洞：一个能产生有效证明的服务商实际上可能只执行了远小于声明模型的计算量，形成了"正确性可证明但工作量不可证明"的逻辑鸿沟。

Method: 1) 形式化定义"努力差距"，指出ZK验证只保证输出-架构-权重的一致性，不绑定计算努力。2) 提出Hollow-LLM攻击：不诚实提供者保持声明架构和参数数量，但嵌入代数结构坍缩的幽灵权重，使有效计算被大幅缩减。3) 刻画与标准Transformer块兼容的具体幽灵权重族。4) 证明这种空心部署在相同验证电路下以零质量损失显著降低服务成本。

Result: 作者展示了幽灵权重的具体构造方式，证明这些权重能够满足验证电路并产生有效证明，同时使提供商的实际计算量等同于比声明模型小得多的规模。空心部署在相同的ZK验证电路下实现了服务成本的大幅降低且零质量损失，形成了不诚实提供商可盈利的均衡状态。

Conclusion: "正确推理的证明"并不等于"大规模模型执行的证明"。ZK推理验证必须辅以额外保护措施，将正确性与可验证的计算工作量绑定，以填补这一努力差距，防止恶意提供商通过幽灵权重虚报模型规模获利。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow in scale and are predominantly served from remote platforms, verifying faithful inference execution becomes critical (i.e., ensuring that a provider actually executes the advertised model and computational workload rather than a tampered or downsized variant). Zero-knowledge (ZK) LLM inference offers an appealing approach. It promises public verifiability and delivers per-instance guarantees of equational correctness by proving that an output is consistent with executing a public architecture under committed, private weights. Though, we show that it does not bind the effort expended to produce the output. In this paper, we formalize this overlooked effort gap and introduce the Hollow-LLM Attack, in which a dishonest provider retains the declared architecture and parameter count but embeds ghost weights whose algebraic structure collapses effective computation. These witnesses satisfy the verification circuit and yield valid proofs, even though the dishonest model owner, who serves as the prover, performs computation commensurate with a much smaller model than the declared public architecture. This creates a profitable equilibrium in which providers deliver provably correct outputs at small-model cost while overclaiming model size. Accordingly, we characterize concrete families of ghost weights that compose with standard transformer blocks and show that such hollow deployments substantially reduce serving cost with zero quality loss under the same verification circuit. These findings underscore that proof of correct inference is not proof of large-model execution and necessitate additional protections to bind correctness to verifiable computational work.

</details>


### [126] [A Biometric Sensor Network to Enable Real-Time Measurement of Individual Student Engagement in STEM Lecture Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28944)
*Ahmed Elsayed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Biometric Sensor Network (BSN)的新型系统，通过分布式学生处理单元(SPUs)实现STEM课堂中学生参与度的实时、非侵入式测量与持续追踪，在保护隐私的前提下融合面部检测、视线估计和情感分析等技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生参与度是影响STEM教育中学术表现和留校率的关键预测因子，但现有测量方法存在侵入性强、人工成本高、不适合实时课堂应用等问题。

Method: 提出BSN架构，由作为分布式传感节点的SPUs组成。每个SPU支持两种模式：(i)数据集收集模式，临时记录原始视频以构建私有SE数据集；(ii)分析模式，对10秒视频片段进行全设备端实时推理（包括人脸检测、视线估计、情感分析），不存储或传输原始帧。系统还包含安全后端基础设施，负责设备认证、会话编排和加密数据摄入。

Result: 系统满足五项设计目标：非侵入性、非侵入性、非污名化、实时性和自动化，同时确保学生数据安全和隐私的严格保护。所有可识别的视频数据均不离开设备。

Conclusion: 该系统整合了硬件设计、计算机视觉管道、无线网络、安全协议和会话级数据管理，为课堂环境中学生参与度的连续、隐私安全的实时测量提供了一套完整解决方案。

Abstract: Student engagement (SE) is a critical predictor of academic performance and retention in STEM education, yet existing measurement approaches are often intrusive, manually intensive, or unsuitable for real-time classroom use. This thesis proposes a novel $\textit{Biometric Sensor Network}$ (BSN) designed to enable real-time measurement and continuous tracking of individual student engagement in STEM classroom environments. The system enables capturing of behavioral, emotional, and cognitive indicators through camera-based sensing while preserving ethical and privacy constraints.
  To measure these indicators unobtrusively and ethically, we propose a BSN composed of $\textit{Student Processing Units}$ (SPUs) that function as distributed sensing nodes. The network is explicitly designed to satisfy five objectives: it must be $\textbf{non-intrusive}, \textbf{non-invasive}, \textbf{non-stigmatizing}, \textbf{real-time}$, and $\textbf{automatic}$, while ensuring rigorous protection of student data security and privacy.
  Each SPU supports two operational modes: (i) a $\textit{dataset-collection mode}$, in which raw student video is temporarily recorded to construct a private SE dataset for model training and validation, and (ii) an $\textit{analysis mode}$, in which the SPU performs real-time inference on 10-second video segments without storing or transmitting raw frames. In this analysis role, each SPU enables fully on-device processing---including face detection, gaze estimation, and affective analysis---ensuring that no identifiable video data leaves the device. A secure backend infrastructure manages device authentication, session orchestration, and encrypted data ingestion. The full system integrates hardware design, computer-vision pipelines, wireless networking, security protocols, and session-level data management.

</details>


### [127] [MESS: Fast and Private Semantic Search on Multi-Graph HNSW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28999)
*Haoyu Cui,Zengpeng Li,Tien Tuan Anh Dinh,Mei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MESS是一个同时实现隐私性、准确性和高效性的语义搜索系统，通过将向量映射为二进制码、应用LSH和随机响应技术，并构建基于扰动码的多图HNSW索引，在保证数据、查询、访问模式和搜索模式隐私的同时，实现了比现有最优方法低15.08倍的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的隐私语义搜索系统通常只采用同态加密（HE）、不经意RAM（ORAM）或差分隐私（DP）中的某一种技术，无法同时满足隐私、准确性和高效性这三个关键属性。当搜索系统托管在可信云提供商处时，数据和查询缺乏隐私保护，因此需要设计一个能同时兼顾这三方面需求的系统。

Method: MESS将原始向量映射为二进制码，应用局部敏感哈希（LSH）和随机响应技术对数据进行扰动，并在扰动后的码上构建多图分层可导航小世界（HNSW）索引。通过两阶段查询扰动机制确保搜索模式隐私，多图索引设计用于缓解扰动对结果质量的影响，搜索直接在扰动码上执行，避免同态加密或ORAM带来的开销。

Result: 实验结果表明，MESS相比现有最先进的基线方法实现了高达15.08倍的延迟降低，同时保持了隐私性和准确性，并给出了系统隐私性的正式分析。

Conclusion: MESS成功证明了通过二进制编码、LSH、随机响应和基于多图HNSW索引的组合设计，可以在不牺牲隐私的前提下实现高效的语义搜索，同时保证结果的准确性，为隐私保护的语义搜索系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic search systems map data to a high-dimensional vector space and support retrieval of similar data via approximate nearest neighbor search. When the system is hosted by a trusted cloud provider, there is no privacy for the data or the query. Our goal is to design a system with three properties: privacy, accuracy, and efficiency. Existing works adopt either homomorphic encryption (HE), oblivious RAM (ORAM), or a differential privacy (DP) approach. They fall short of achieving all three properties.
  In this paper, we present MESS, a system that realizes our goal. It maps the original vectors into binary codes, applies locality-sensitive hashing (LSH) and randomized response, and constructs a multi-graph Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) index over the perturbed codes. MESS ensures data, query, and access pattern privacy. It also ensures search pattern privacy via a two-phase query perturbation mechanism. The multi-graph index mitigates the impact of perturbation on result quality, thereby achieving accuracy. MESS is efficient because search is performed directly over perturbed codes, without the overhead of homomorphic encryption or ORAM. We give formal analysis of the system's privacy and extensive evaluation of its performance. The results show that MESS achieves up to 15.08\times lower latency than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [128] [GoldenRetriever: Non-Interactive Homomorphic Encrypted Retrieval for Privacy-Preserving RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29019)
*Yang Gao,Gang Quan,Scott Piersall,Qian Lou,Dongdong Wang,Liqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于阈值选择的非交互式加密检索框架，用于保护RAG系统中的隐私。通过用阈值选择替代top-k排序，将计算复杂度从二次降为线性，并基于CKKS同态加密实现全加密的相似度评估和文档选择，同时引入精度稳定的掩码极化方法解决近似加密计算与离散token重建之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG管道处理明文数据引发隐私问题；已有的隐私保护检索方法（如同态加密和私有信息检索）依赖交互式协议或基于排序的选择机制，导致高延迟和潜在信息泄露风险。

Method: 提出基于阈值选择的非交互式加密检索框架：1) 用相似度分数超过预定义阈值的文档选择替代昂贵的top-k排序，将计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)；2) 采用CKKS同态加密实现全加密的相似度评估与文档选择；3) 提出精度稳定的掩码极化方法，弥合近似加密计算与离散token重建之间的差距。

Result: 在标准检索基准上的实验表明，该方法在实现有竞争力的检索效果的同时，相比基于排序的加密方法显著降低了延迟。

Conclusion: 阈值选择可作为可扩展且安全的RAG系统的实用基础，兼顾隐私保护与效率。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances large language models by incorporating external knowledge, but existing pipelines typically operate on plaintext data, raising significant privacy concerns. Prior work on privacy-preserving retrieval leverages cryptographic techniques such as homomorphic encryption (HE) and private information retrieval (PIR), but often relies on interactive protocols or ranking-based selection mechanisms that incur high latency and potential information leakage. In this paper, we propose a practical non-interactive encrypted retrieval framework for RAG based on threshold selection. Instead of performing expensive top-$k$ ranking under encryption, our approach selects documents whose similarity scores exceed a predefined threshold, reducing computational complexity from quadratic to linear in the corpus size. We implement this design using CKKS-based homomorphic computation, enabling fully encrypted similarity evaluation and document selection without revealing query content, intermediate scores, or selected indices. To bridge the gap between approximate encrypted computation and discrete token reconstruction, we introduce a precision-stable mask polarization method that ensures accurate recovery of selected documents. Experiments on standard retrieval benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive retrieval effectiveness while significantly reducing latency compared to ranking-based encrypted methods. These results highlight threshold-based selection as a practical foundation for scalable and secure RAG systems.

</details>


### [129] [Memory Provenance Laundering in LLM Agents: A Non-Amplification Firewall for Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29167)
*Jinghan Xu,Yiyong Xiao,Wanru Shao,Hankai Liu,Xinjin Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文识别了大语言模型智能体长期记忆中的"记忆来源洗白"攻击：外部不可信观测在记忆整合时被重写为看似可信的用户历史或工作流依据，从而保留动作触发条件却抹去低信任来源。作者提出PPMF（Provenance-Preserving Memory Firewall），一种轻量级记忆中间件，通过保留平台维护的来源信息并根据记忆来源权限匹配动作风险来授权工具调用，有效阻止未授权的高风险动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体利用长期记忆复用先前偏好与工作流，但这种机制会把不可信的外部观测转化为持久的动作上下文。现有的提示过滤器、内容消毒器和工具防护都未能在有损记忆整合后强制执行"来源权限不放大"原则，导致安全漏洞。

Method: 形式化定义记忆来源洗白的边界，设计PPMF中间件：保留平台维护的来源(provenance)信息，通过将动作风险与动作相关记忆的来源权限进行匹配来授权工具调用，并结合确认机制与风险标签实现细粒度控制。

Result: 在schema驱动的评估中，易受攻击的记忆整合可达100%的攻击成功率(ASR)；而在保留平台来源、确认机制和风险标签后，所有未授权的危险动作均被PPMF拦截，确认的正常动作和定向的低风险记忆使用仍可正常执行。

Conclusion: PPMF证明了通过保留来源信息和基于来源权限的授权机制，可以有效阻止LLM智能体长期记忆系统遭受来源洗白攻击，且不会影响正常动作的执行。

Abstract: Long-term memory lets large language model(LLM) agents reuse prior preferences and work flows, but it also turns untrusted observations into persistent action context. We identify memory provenance laundering: during LLM-based memory consolidation, an external observation may be rewritten as apparent user history or workflow support, preserving an action trigger while erasing the low-trust source that should limit its authority. Existing prompt filters, content sanitizers, and tool guards do not enforce source-authority non-amplification after lossy memory consolidation. We formalize this boundary and instantiate it as Provenance-Preserving Memory Fire wall (PPMF), a lightweight memory middleware that preserves platform-maintained provenance and authorizes tool calls by matching action risk to the authority of action-relevant memories. In our schema-grounded evaluation with fixed risk policies, vulnerable consolidated memories reach up to 1.000 attack success rate(ASR); with intact platform-maintained provenance, confirmation, and risk labels, no evaluated unauthorized high-risk action passes the PPMF gate while confirmed benign actions and targeted low-risk memory use remain executable.

</details>


### [130] [Alignment Is Local: A Paired Diagnostic for GUI Agents under User-Side Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29199)
*Haoxin An,Yunpeng Song,Zihao Bai,Zhongmin Cai,Guojun Xiong,Chenhao Lin,Wentao Chen,Feng Wei,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示GUI智能体的提示级对齐是"局部现象"：仅在单轮、显式表达意图的评测切片中可靠，在深度交互与隐蔽威胁下系统性退化。通过配对诊断实验（无环境注入的用户侧说服），证明单行护栏可将单轮ASR降低约40个百分点，但四轮升级链会使护栏后的ASR反弹约20个百分点；不同模型的脆弱性来源不同（Qwen为护栏侵蚀，Claude/GPT为与防御无关的动态风险），且在护栏存在时隐蔽请求反而比显式请求更易成功。静态单轮ASR系统性高估了实际部署鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GUI智能体的主流轻量防御手段（提示级对齐）仅在狭窄评测条件下表现良好，无法在真实动态交互和精确威胁条件下保持可信赖性。作者旨在证明这种防御在用户可轻易跨越的两个维度（多轮升级、隐蔽表达）上系统性退化，从而揭露静态评测对部署鲁棒性的高估问题。

Method: 采用"配对诊断"方法，在三个前沿GUI智能体（Qwen、Claude、GPT）上实施屏幕端、用户侧说服攻击（无环境注入）。设置两类对比：独立探测vs四轮升级链；显式意图vs隐蔽意图。考查单行护栏在单轮与多轮情境下的ASR（成功攻击率）和过度拒绝成本差异。

Result: ① 单行护栏在单轮独立探测下可使ASR降低约40个百分点，过度拒绝几乎为零；② 从独立探测转为四轮升级链后，所有模型护栏后的ASR回升约20个百分点；③ 回升原因因模型而异：Qwen主要由护栏侵蚀导致，Claude和GPT则主要由与防御正交的动态风险导致；④ 护栏下的显著性缺口符号翻转：无护栏时隐蔽请求并无系统性优势，有护栏时隐蔽请求反而更成功，说明护栏仅在意图被命名时有效。

Conclusion: 静态单轮ASR评测会以系统性且可预测的幅度高估GUI智能体的实际部署鲁棒性。提示级对齐不足以作为普适的安全防御手段，需发展能够应对多轮交互和隐蔽威胁的更强对齐机制，评测范式也应从单轮静态指标转向动态多轮评估。

Abstract: Trustworthy deployment of GUI agents in ubiquitous computing settings requires alignment that survives dynamic interaction and precise threat conditions, not just single-turn refusal of explicit harmful requests. We argue that prompt-level alignment, the dominant lightweight defense in current mobile agents, is a local phenomenon: it works reliably only in the narrow evaluation slice where it is typically measured, namely single-turn, explicitly-verbalized intent, and degrades systematically along two axes that any real user can traverse. Using a paired diagnostic on three frontier GUI agents, screen-grounded, user-side persuasion, with no environment injection, we show that a one-line guardrail achieves large single-shot ASR reductions, up to roughly 40 points, at near-zero over-refusal cost. Nevertheless, moving from independent probes to four-turn escalation chains raises guarded ASR by approximately 20 points on every model. Relative to the neutral baselines, this increase reflects substantial guardrail erosion for Qwen but a largely defense-orthogonal dynamic risk for Claude and GPT. The sign of the salience gap flips under the guardrail: concealed requests are not systematically more successful than explicit ones without a guardrail, but are more successful with one, indicating that the defense engages primarily when intent is named. Static single-turn ASR therefore overstates deployed robustness by a systematic and predictable margin.

</details>


### [131] [MOSAIC: Masked Outsourcing of Secure AI Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29221)
*James Hsin-yu Chiang,Sheila Zingg,Kari Kostiainen,Srdjan Capkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了MOSAIC，一种安全的AI计算外包协议，通过新颖的矩阵乘法掩码协议和基于Hadamard旋转的误差缩放机制，使计算能力弱的可信客户端可以将大型Transformer推理（如70B模型）安全外包给不可信服务器，同时实现最优的渐近客户端开销和比先前工作快数个数量级的运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信但计算能力弱的客户端需要将AI计算外包给不可信但强大的服务器，同时客户端持有输入和模型，服务器不能学习二者。先前工作无法扩展到现代大规模工作负载（如大型Transformer推理），需要更高效的协议。

Method: 核心是新颖的矩阵乘法掩码协议，通过向乘法结果引入少量噪声并放宽正确性要求，实现最优渐近客户端开销。针对噪声在Transformer多层中累积的问题，提出基于随机Hadamard旋转的误差缩放机制来控制误差增长。安全性归约到判定性LWE和LPN假设。

Result: 在70B参数Transformer模型上，MOSAIC的困惑度与流行的量化方法相当，在HumanEval上甚至匹配全精度BF16推理。具体运行时间比先前工作快数个数量级，能够扩展至远超先前工作的矩阵规模。

Conclusion: MOSAIC通过在数据中心中保持较小的可信计算基（TCB）并将大部分AI计算外包给不可信加速器，展示了大规模机密AI的可行路径，可无缝适配现代数据中心中已分布化的非机密推理架构。

Abstract: We address the challenge of securely and efficiently outsourcing AI computations from a trusted but computationally weak client to an untrusted but powerful server, in the setting where the client holds both the input and the model, and the server must learn neither. We present MOSAIC, whose core is a novel matrix-multiplication masking protocol that scales to far larger matrices than prior work, enabling the safe outsourcing of modern workloads such as large transformer inference. By introducing small amounts of noise to the multiplication result and thereby relaxing correctness, MOSAIC achieves optimal asymptotic client overhead and concrete runtimes orders of magnitude faster than prior work. Its security reduces to the decisional LWE and LPN assumptions.
  Because this noise accumulates across the many layers of a transformer, a key technical challenge is bounding error growth; MOSAIC addresses this with an error-scaling mechanism based on random Hadamard rotations. On large 70B transformer models, MOSAIC's perplexity is comparable to popular quantization approaches and even matches full-precision BF16 inference on HumanEval.
  Finally, we present an end-to-end implementation showing how ideas like MOSAIC can promise a path towards large-scale confidential AI in modern data centers. Non-confidential inference is already distributed across phase (prefill/decode), layer, and time to maximize utilization of heterogeneous hardware, using RDMA-like networking to move activations, cached KV values, and weights across nodes. MOSAIC enables scaling of confidential compute by keeping the trusted computing base (TCB) small and outsourcing the bulk of the AI computation to untrusted accelerators.

</details>


### [132] [JUNO: Aggregated Vector Consensus for Optimal Asynchronous Common Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29244)
*Liangrong Zhao,Qin Wang,Joseph K. Liu,Jiangshan Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的异步网络下的向量共识原语——聚合向量共识，并基于此实现了异步公共子集协议Juno，实现了最优的O(n²)消息复杂度。与HoneyBadgerBFT和Dory相比，Juno的吞吐量分别提升93%和47%，填补了向量共识协议在全异步网络中应用的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量共识协议在完全异步网络环境下的应用存在明显空白，且已有的异步公共子集协议（如HoneyBadgerBFT、Dory）在通信复杂度和性能上仍有提升空间。作者旨在设计一种新的向量共识原语，并将其落地为高效协议，以弥补这一研究缺口。

Method: 作者首先提出了聚合向量共识（aggregated vector consensus）原语，它通过输出由独立进程输入聚合而成的值向量来实现共识。在此基础上，设计了Juno协议，该协议完整实现了所提出的聚合向量共识，并达到了最优的O(n²)消息复杂度。随后将Juno与HoneyBadgerBFT和Dory进行了实现与对比评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，Juno的平均吞吐量相比HoneyBadgerBFT提升93%，相比Dory提升47%，验证了该协议在效率和性能上的显著优势。

Conclusion: 该研究成功填补了向量共识协议在完全异步网络环境下应用的研究空白，提出的聚合向量共识原语及其实现Juno协议在消息复杂度和实际吞吐性能上均表现优异，为该方向后续研究提供了有力基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose \textit{aggregated vector consensus}, a new vector consensus primitive designed for asynchronous networks. The primitive achieves agreement by outputting a vector of values aggregated from independent process inputs. We then introduce \textsc{Juno}, an asynchronous common subset (ACS) protocol that fully implements our aggregated vector consensus to attain optimal $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ message complexity.
  We further implement and evaluate \textsc{Juno} in comparison with the legacy HoneyBadgerBFT and the state-of-the-art Dory. Experiment results demonstrate its efficacy and efficiency. Our protocol demonstrates an average throughput performance improvement of 93\% compared with HoneyBadgerBFT and a 47\% improvement compared with Dory. Notably, our study makes significant progress in addressing the gap in applying vector consensus protocol in fully asynchronous networks.

</details>


### [133] [On the Resilience of 5G NR Against Jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29384)
*Sotiris Michaelides,Fidelius Lula,Daniel Eguiguren Chavez,Michael Rademacher,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文在ns-3模拟器中实现并开源了一个5G干扰器，用于系统性地比较不同5G物理层配置（包括蜂窝技术、频率、子载波间隔和信道带宽）对干扰韧性的影响。结果显示蜂窝技术和子载波间隔对干扰韧性影响不大，而信道带宽和频率范围是提升干扰韧性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G网络在关键基础设施和工业网络中的应用日益广泛，但其对蜂窝干扰的韧性缺乏系统性理解。现有研究仅在固定5G物理层配置下进行孤立评估，难以进行可靠的横向比较（如不同频段或信道带宽之间的干扰韧性差异），因此需要一种可对比、可控的评估方法。

Method: 基于ns-3模拟器实现并开源了一个5G干扰器模型，利用模拟的可控确定性，在多种维度下系统比较蜂窝干扰的影响，包括蜂窝技术、频率、子载波间隔和信道带宽等参数。

Result: 研究发现蜂窝技术和子载波间隔对干扰韧性的影响较小，而信道带宽和频率范围才是决定干扰韧性的关键因素，即在提升5G抗干扰能力方面起主导作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为5G网络干扰韧性的系统性评估提供了开源模拟工具和基准结果，明确了影响干扰韧性的关键物理层参数，有助于指导5G在关键任务场景中的部署与优化。

Abstract: With the increasing use of 5G networks in availability-critical systems, including industrial networks and critical infrastructure, a comprehensive understanding of their resilience to cellular jamming has become imperative. However, research so far has focused on isolated evaluations under fixed 5G physical-layer configurations, making it difficult to perform sound comparisons, for example, to identify differences between frequency bands or channel bandwidths w.r.t. jamming resilience. To fill this gap, we implement and release an open-source 5G jammer within the ns-3 simulator. Leveraging the controllable determinism of simulation, we compare the impact of cellular jamming across different dimensions such as cellular technologies, frequencies, subcarrier spacing, and channel bandwidth. Our results show that while cellular technology and subcarrier spacing have little impact, channel bandwidth and frequency range play a critical role in improving jamming resilience.

</details>


### [134] [On fair and realistic performance evaluations for graph-based lateral movement detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29390)
*Corentin Larroche*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文调研了横向移动检测研究中两个流行基准数据集的前处理与标注方法，发现不同论文间存在显著差异，并提出了一套有依据的前处理和标注策略，在该策略下重新评估三个经典检测方法，结果与原论文显著不同，凸显了数据前处理和标注实践对评估公平性与真实性的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向移动检测研究依赖基准数据集进行评估，但不同论文对数据的前处理方式和横向移动事件标注方式差异很大，导致评估结果的公平性和真实性存疑。作者希望系统性地调查这种不一致性，并展示其对下游评估的影响。

Method: 作者首先调查了两个流行基准数据集在文献中前处理和标注方法的差异；然后提出了有充分依据的前处理与标注策略；最后在统一的新策略下重新评估了三个被广泛引用的横向移动检测方法。

Result: 重新评估三个广泛引用的方法后，其结果与原论文报告的结果存在显著差异，进一步证明了数据集前处理和标注方式对横向移动检测评估结果的重大影响。

Conclusion: 数据集前处理和标注实践在横向移动检测器评估中至关重要，标准化这些实践对确保评估的公平性与真实性十分必要，同时也对既有文献结果的可复现性提出了警示。

Abstract: Research on lateral movement detection has made significant progress in recent years, spurred by the widespread availability of benchmark datasets that make evaluating detectors practical. However, the exact way in which these benchmark datasets are used varies across the literature: both the preprocessing applied before feeding the data to the detector and the labeling of lateral movement-related events change substantially from one paper to another. We survey preprocessing and labeling methodologies for two popular datasets and demonstrate their impact on the fairness and realism of downstream evaluations. We also propose well-grounded preprocessing and labeling policies for these datasets. Finally, we re-evaluate three widely cited lateral movement detection methods under these new policies; our results differ significantly from those reported in the original papers, further highlighting the critical importance of dataset preprocessing and labeling practices in evaluating lateral movement detectors.

</details>


### [135] [Enforcing Cryptographic Distributed-VCS Access Control with No Trust on Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29417)
*Xin Xu,Zhen Yang,Quanwei Cai,Jingqiang Lin,Liangqin Ren,Bo Chen,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DVAC，一种基于属性基加密（ABE）和属性基签名（ABS）的去中心化版本控制系统访问控制方案，无需中心服务器即可在分布式节点上实现文件粒度的读写分离访问控制，并通过以太坊智能合约维护访问控制策略，已与Git集成并验证开销适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VCS访问控制方案依赖中心服务器，无法在完全分布式场景中部署；且难以对写权限实施细粒度访问控制，无法满足分布式环境中的协作需求。

Method: DVAC基于属性基加密（ABE）和属性基签名（ABS）实现分布式节点上的密码学访问控制；设计了版本控制适配协议以保证版本控制核心功能完整性和数据安全；利用以太坊智能合约去中心化存储和管理访问控制策略；采用模块化架构可与现有DVCS（如Git）无缝集成。

Result: 实现了DVAC原型并与Git集成，通过全面性能评估证明其引入的开销适中。

Conclusion: DVAC能够在无中心服务器支持下实现文件粒度的读写分离访问控制，同时兼顾版本控制功能完整性与数据安全性，且集成代价小、性能开销可控。

Abstract: Version control systems (VCS), including central VCS (CVCS) and distributed VCS (DVCS), are widely adopted to manage changes to software code and various types of documents. Unlike CVCS, where entities obtain data from a central server, each entity in DVCS stores the entire repository and shares it independently. In VCS, existing access control schemes require the participation of a central server and cannot be deployed in a completely distributed scenario. Additionally, these schemes often fail to enforce fine-grained access control for write permissions, which is crucial for collaborative work in a distributed environment. In this paper, we propose a distributed version control system access control scheme (named DVAC), which enforces cryptographic access control on distributed user nodes based on attribute-based encryption (ABE) and attribute-based signature (ABS). DVAC is designed to enforce a cryptographic access control protocol for DVCS, which enables file granularity read and write separation access control without the support of a central server. To ensure the integrity of the core version control functions in DVCS while protecting data security, DVAC incorporates a version control adaptation protocol. Additionally, DVAC leverages Ethereum smart contracts to maintain access control policies, ensuring distributed storage and trusted management of access policies. The architecture of DVAC is designed to seamlessly integrate with existing mature DVCS, such as Git, with minimal modifications. We have implemented a prototype of DVAC and integrated it with Git. A comprehensive performance evaluation was conducted to assess the overhead introduced by DVAC, and it was demonstrated that the overhead is modest.

</details>


### [136] [Bending the Curve: Operational Cyber Epidemiology for Ransomware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29444)
*Stephen V Flowerday,Nikolay Lipskiy,Steven Furnell,Callum E Flowerday,John Hale*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个操作性的网络流行病学框架，将SEIR传染病模型适配到勒索软件事件管理中，将Exposed定义为潜伏性入侵和筹备阶段（包括停留期），Infectious定义为活跃横向传播，并基于ISO 5477:2023和UNDRR-ISC 2025指南定义了可互操作的勒索软件病例定义和关键信息要素（EEI），利用R0和Re作为安全运营中心的近实时方向性决策辅助工具，从WannaCry、NotPetya、SolarWinds、MGM和Caesars等公开事件中验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件通常被视为检测问题，但最具破坏性的攻击更像疫情爆发——单一立足点可通过身份、管理工具和共享服务蔓延，而响应人员需在信息不完整的情况下做出时间紧迫的决策。现有方法缺少一个能将技术遥测与资源约束下的遏制决策联系起来的共享语言。

Method: 将SEIR流行病学模型适配为勒索软件事件管理本体，区分Exposed（潜在入侵/筹备/停留期）与Infectious（活跃横向传播），并分离传播状态与观测状态以避免混淆传播动态与检测能力；依据ISO 5477:2023公共卫生应急准备与响应信息管理指南和2025 UNDRR-ISC灾害信息档案定义互操作性病例定义和关键信息要素（EEI）；使用基本/有效再生数R0和Re作为方向性决策辅助，推导将Re降至1以下的操作性保护阈值启发式方法，并提供与具体工具无关的作战手册卡片。

Result: 通过WannaCry、NotPetya、SolarWinds、MGM和Caesars等公开报道的事件案例，展示了疫情式度量方法如何支持更早的隔离、凭据遏制和恢复排序；论文提供了实用的保护阈值启发式规则和工具无关的行动触发机制。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是建立一种共享语言，将技术遥测与资源约束下的遏制决策相连接，为安全运营中心提供近实时的方向性决策辅助工具，弥补勒索软件事件管理中检测视图与响应决策之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Ransomware is often treated as a detection problem, but the most disruptive incidents behave more like outbreaks. A single foothold can spread through identities, administrative tools, and shared services while responders make time-critical decisions with incomplete visibility. This paper presents an operational cyber epidemiology framework that adapts the Susceptible-Exposed-Infectious-Removed (SEIR) model to ransomware incident management. In this ontology, Exposed denotes latent compromise and staging, including the dwell period before confirmed secondary compromise, while Infectious denotes active lateral propagation. Drawing on ISO 5477:2023 guidance for public health emergency preparedness and response information management and the 2025 UNDRR-ISC Hazard Information Profiles, the framework defines interoperable ransomware case definitions and Essential Elements of Information for cross-incident comparison. Basic and effective reproduction numbers, R0 and Re, are used as directional, near-real-time decision aids for security operations centers. Propagation state is separated from observation status to avoid confusing spread dynamics with detection capability. Publicly reported incidents, including WannaCry, NotPetya, SolarWinds, and MGM and Caesars, illustrate how outbreak-style measures can support earlier isolation, credential containment, and restoration sequencing. The paper also derives practical protection-threshold heuristics aimed at reducing Re below 1 and provides a tool-agnostic playbook card linking operational information to explicit action triggers. The primary contribution is a shared language that connects technical telemetry to containment decisions under resource constraints.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Resilience: Antifragility in Critical Infrastructure Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29550)
*Stephen Flowerday,Mauricio Papa,Ethan Flowerday*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向关键基础设施（CI）网络安全的反脆弱性理论（AFT），基于五状态弹性系统模型和Jensen增益/灾后增益的数学定义，并结合CISSM网络事件数据库与HAI工业控制数据集进行了两层实证检验，为未来反脆弱性测试确立了"差异化脆弱性负担"与"过程级扰动可观测性"两大前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施的网络安全日益需要超越"恢复"、走向"在扰动下有界改进"的框架，但针对操作技术（OT）领域的经验性理论仍然有限，亟需填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用两层实证设计：第一层使用CISSM网络事件数据库中与CI相关的子集，检验OT邻近部门的事件特征；第二层使用HAI硬件在环工业控制数据集，验证攻击标签下观测值的偏差分布与连续攻击窗口内的过程状态偏差变化趋势。同时利用Jensen增益和灾后增益给出有界的数学定义。

Result: OT邻近部门中破坏性或混合事件占比显著高于对照部门（65.3%对46.8%，p<0.001），且物理攻击和数据攻击子类型更集中。HAI中攻击标签观测值超过基线偏差第95百分位的可能性是正常观测的7.43倍（p<0.001）。连续攻击窗口内过程状态偏差均值显著下降（Spearman rho=-0.688, p=0.007），表明存在可测量的响应变化而非自适应增益的直接证明。

Conclusion: 研究成果为反脆弱性测试确立了两个前提条件：差异化的脆弱性负担以及过程级扰动的可观测性，为关键基础设施网络安全的后续反脆弱性验证奠定了理论基础和实证基础。

Abstract: Critical infrastructure cybersecurity increasingly requires frameworks that move beyond recovery toward bounded improvement under disruption, yet empirically grounded theories for operational technology remain limited. This paper develops a Theory of Antifragility (AFT) for critical infrastructure (CI) cybersecurity, anchored in a five-state Resilient System Model and a bounded mathematical definition based on Jensen gain and post-disruption gain. A two-layer empirical design pairs a CI-relevant subset of the CISSM Cyber Events Database with the HAI hardware-in-the-loop industrial control dataset and tests three confirmatory hypotheses and one exploratory proposition. OT-adjacent sectors show significantly higher shares of disruptive or mixed events than comparison sectors (65.3 percent versus 46.8 percent, p less than 0.001), together with a greater concentration of physical-attack and data-attack subtypes. In HAI, attack-labeled observations were 7.43 times more likely than normal observations to exceed the 95th percentile of baseline deviation (p less than 0.001). Across successive attack windows, mean process-state deviation declined significantly (Spearman rho = -0.688, p = 0.007), indicating measurable response variation rather than proof of adaptive gain. Together, the findings establish two prerequisites for future antifragility testing: differentiated fragility burden and process-level perturbation observability.

</details>


### [138] [CWEEP: A Lexical Static Analysis Framework for CWE Early Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29604)
*Bryan Kwan,Benjamin Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CWEEP是一个用于检测寄存器传输级（RTL）代码安全弱点的静态分析框架。它不需要详细的安全规格说明，能够在RTL开发早期使用，准确定位潜在漏洞在RTL中的具体位置，并在适用时提供自动代码修复建议。在两个SoC设计（含手动插入的bug）及一个由大语言模型生成的3874个有缺陷模块的数据集上，CWEEP的正确警告率达到60.8%，而此前工作的工具在同一数据集上仅为17.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着硬件层成为攻击者的重点目标，改进硬件安全验证技术的需求日益迫切。现有的安全验证技术需要具备安全专业知识的人员进行大量手工操作，且缺乏标准方法来定位寄存器传输级（RTL）代码中的故障位置。

Method: CWEEP是一种静态分析框架，用于检测RTL代码中的安全弱点。它不需要详细的安全规格说明，因此可以在RTL开发的早期阶段、属性仍在构建时使用。CWEEP能够精确定位RTL中潜在漏洞所在的位置，并在适用时提供自动代码修复建议。

Result: 使用文献数据集，在两组分别包含手动插入bug的SoC设计以及大语言模型生成的3874个有缺陷模块的数据集上进行评估。CWEEP在60.8%的情况下发出正确警告，而先前工作的工具在同一数据集上的正确警告率仅为17.5%。

Conclusion: CWEEP作为一种无需安全规格说明的静态分析框架，能够显著优于先前工作，在RTL安全弱点的检测定位和修复建议方面展现出明显优势，特别适合在RTL开发早期使用。

Abstract: As the hardware layer becomes a focus point for attackers, the need for improved hardware security verification techniques is more important than ever. State-of-the-art security verification techniques require significant manual effort from individuals with security expertise. Furthermore, there is no standard method to locate where the fault lies within the register transfer level (RTL) code. This paper presents CWEEP, a static analysis framework for detecting security weaknesses in RTL. CWEEP does not require a detailed security specification, so it can be used in the early stages of RTL development while properties are still under construction. Furthermore, CWEEP can identify the exact location in the RTL where the potential vulnerability resides and supports automatic code repair suggestions when applicable. Using datasets from the literature, we evaluate the performance of CWEEP on a set of two SoC designs with manually inserted bugs and on a large language model generated dataset, consisting of 3874 buggy modules. We find that CWEEP issues a correct warning up to 60.8% of the time. In contrast, the tool from a previous work issued a correct warning 17.5% of the time for the same dataset.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Simple-regret rates and minimax optimality of fixed-prior expected improvement in Matérn and squared-exponential RKHSs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29245)
*Emmanuel Vazquez,Sébastien Petit*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了确定性目标函数最小化中的期望改进（EI）策略。在RKHS假设下，EI策略在有限预算内的简单遗憾界被导出：对于各向同性Matérn核为O(N^{-ν/d})，对于平方指数核为指数速率。当精确最大化EI时，平方指数核下的速率为O(exp[-c_2 N^{1/d} log(eN)])。该策略在Matérn核下达到极小极大最优，在平方指数核下达到指数常数内的极小极大最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期望改进（EI）是贝叶斯优化中最广泛使用的获得函数之一，但对其在确定性目标函数上的理论保证（特别是有限预算下的遗憾界和极小极大最优性）缺乏严格分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，从信息论的创新空间视角为EI策略建立严谨的收敛速率分析。

Method: 本文采用特征空间分析框架：将候选点x处的归一化后验标准差识别为规范特征空间中对应创新的范数（即k(x,·)与先前评估表示张成空间正交的分量）。通过Gram行列式和不同维数子空间的Kolmogorov宽度来估计顺序分离半径，从而界定任意查询序列中的排序创新范数。将这些估计与一步遗憾不等式结合，得到有限预算下的简单遗憾界。

Result: 在N次初始后查询后，各向同性Matérn核（平滑度ν>0）的简单遗憾为O(N^{-ν/d})；各向同性平方指数核的简单遗憾为O(exp[-c_1 min{N, N^{1/d} log(eN)}])，在精确EI最大化下为O(exp[-c_2 N^{1/d} log(eN)])。这些界对RKHS球半径B≥0一致成立。对于具有非空内部的X和B>0，精确EI策略在Matérn核下达到极小极大最优，在平方指数核下达到指数常数内的极小极大最优。

Conclusion: 通过将EI策略与特征空间创新结构建立联系，本文证明了EI策略不仅在有限预算下具有明确的收敛速率保证，而且在Matérn核下是极小极大最优的，在平方指数核下是最优（相差指数常数）。这为EI作为贝叶斯优化中默认策略的广泛使用提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the expected improvement (EI) policy for minimizing a deterministic objective function $f$ on a nonempty compact set $\mathcal X \subset\mathbb R^d$. We assume that $f$ belongs to the RKHS $\mathcal H_k$ of a continuous positive-semidefinite kernel $k$ on $\mathcal X$. Function values are observed exactly, and EI is computed from a fixed zero-mean Gaussian-process model with covariance $σ^2k$. After an initial design, the policy queries a point whose EI is at least a fixed positive fraction of its maximum.
  We identify the normalized posterior standard deviation at a candidate point $x$ with the norm of the corresponding innovation in the canonical feature space, namely the component of $k(x,\cdot)$ orthogonal to the span of the preceding evaluation representers. Sequential separation radii bound the ranked innovation norms along arbitrary query sequences. We estimate these radii using Gram determinants and Kolmogorov widths for subspaces of different dimensions, then combine the estimates with a one-step regret inequality to obtain finite-budget bounds for simple regret.
  After $N$ post-initial queries, simple regret is $O(N^{-ν/d})$ for isotropic Matérn kernels of smoothness $ν>0$. For the isotropic squared-exponential kernel, simple regret is $O(\exp[-c_1\min\{N, N^{1/d}\log(eN)\}])$ for some $c_1>0$. With exact EI maximization, it is $O(\exp[-c_2N^{1/d} \log(eN)])$ for some $c_2>0$. For every fixed $B\geq0$, these bounds are uniform over the RKHS ball of radius $B$.
  If $\mathcal X$ has nonempty interior and $B>0$, then, among deterministic methods whose final recommendation may be any point of $\mathcal X$, the exact EI policy is minimax-rate optimal over the RKHS ball of radius $B$ for Matérn kernels and minimax-rate optimal up to constants in the exponent for squared-exponential kernels.

</details>


### [140] [Accelerated Random-Sweep Gibbs Sampling for Gaussian Graphical Models via Dual Normal Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28706)
*Borna Khodabandeh,Mehdi Molkaraie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究基于薄膜先验的高斯图模型中随机扫描吉布斯采样的收敛性，证明通过对法向因子图进行傅里叶变换得到的对偶模型中，吉布斯采样收敛速度显著加快。作者推导了齐次k-正则图的精确收敛率，证明所有含环的齐次模型的收敛率在图拓扑上是普适的，并受图的代数连通性控制，同时建立了原模型与对偶模型协方差结构之间的显式代数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吉布斯采样是高斯图模型中最常用的推断方法之一，但其收敛速度往往受图拓扑结构影响且难以分析。传统上对吉布斯采样的收敛性分析都局限在原始域中，而随机扫描吉布斯采样在复杂图结构上的收敛率缺乏精确的理论刻画。本文试图通过对偶变换（傅里叶变换）找到一种新的视角，在保持计算复杂度不变的前提下加速收敛，并给出精确的理论保证。

Method: 首先对高斯图模型的法向因子图应用傅里叶变换，将对偶模型中的局部因子转化为频域表示。然后分别推导原模型和对偶模型中随机扫描吉布斯采样的精确收敛率（针对齐次k-正则图）。对于一般含环的齐次图，证明对偶域中的收敛率是普适的，与图拓扑无关，而由图的代数连通性（Fiedler特征值）决定。此外，建立原模型与对偶模型协方差结构的显式代数关系，从而可以直接从对偶模型的边缘统计量恢复原模型的统计量。

Result: 推导了齐次k-正则图在原域和对偶域的精确收敛率；证明了对偶域中所有含环齐次模型的收敛率具有图拓扑无关的普适性；发现有效收敛率由图的代数连通性控制，实现额外加速且不增加每轮扫描的计算复杂度；建立了原模型与对偶模型之间协方差结构的显式代数关系；数值实验在多种图族上验证了理论结果并显示了显著的收敛率提升。

Conclusion: 对偶域（傅里叶域）是加速吉布斯采样收敛的有效框架，不仅提供了普适的收敛率保证（在齐次含环图中与拓扑无关），还通过代数连通性揭示了进一步加速的途径。这一工作为高斯图模型中的高效推断提供了新的理论工具和实用方法。

Abstract: We study the convergence properties of the random-sweep Gibbs sampler for Gaussian graphical models with a thin-membrane prior. We demonstrate that the convergence rate of the Gibbs sampler is significantly accelerated in the dual model, which is obtained by applying the Fourier transform to the local factors of the normal factor graph representing the original model. In both domains, we derive the exact convergence rates for homogeneous $k$-regular graphs. We prove that, for all homogeneous models whose graphical representations contain cycles, the convergence rate in the dual domain is universal and independent of the underlying graph topology. Moreover, we show that the effective convergence rate in the dual domain is governed by the algebraic connectivity of the graph, providing an additional acceleration without increasing the computational complexity per sweep. We further establish an explicit algebraic relation between the covariance structures of the primal and dual models, enabling marginal statistics of the primal model to be recovered directly from those of the dual model. Finally, numerical experiments on several graph families confirm our theoretical results and demonstrate substantial improvements in the convergence rates in various settings.

</details>


### [141] [Conditioning Tree-Based Diffusions and Flows for Probabilistic Tabular Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28864)
*Silas Koemen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffGBM，一种基于树的扩散/流匹配模型，用于表格回归的条件分布建模。论文揭示了梯度提升集成求解的本质上是一个监督回归问题，其条件机制决定了扩散默认配置的效果。DiffGBM沿两个轴向改进：(1) 高斯路径流匹配训练器，直接学习速度场并代数恢复得分，支持少步确定性ODE采样；(2) "得分灵活"（score-flex）空间，将残差化、EDM风格预条件、log-sigma时间采样、噪声水平特征、损失加权和直方图分辨率作为联合可调轴，而非固定搭配。在11个表格基准上，fold-0调参、fold-1至5评估、匹配40次试验预算和采样器条件下，选定配置在每 个数据集上都优于发布基线（配对Wilcoxon 11/0, p<10^-3），最佳聚合CRPS技能为0.725 vs 0.699。流匹配是最便宜的采样器（比公布基线快5.2倍）且校准最佳，而score-flex则用随机采样器换取精度。非扩散调优基线仍能在个别数据集上获胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于树的扩散模型在没有神经密度估计器的情况下，可以通过拟合灵活的条件预测分布来处理表格回归任务。然而，这类模型继承了神经扩散模型的默认设计选择（包括噪声路径、参数化、训练分布、特征和数据采样器）。作者认为这些默认配置实际上构成了模型的性能约束——梯度提升集成真正解决的是一个监督回归问题，而扩散过程中的条件机制决定了该问题的求解方式。因此，本文致力于将这些隐含的默认选择显式化，从而提升树的扩散模型在表格回归中的性能。

Method: DiffGBM包含两个核心贡献。第一，采用高斯路径流匹配训练器对条件分布p(y|x)建模，直接学习速度场（velocity field），并通过代数运算恢复得分（score），从而支持少步确定性ODE采样。第二，提出"score-flex"空间，将残差化（residualization）、EDM风格预条件、log-sigma时间采样、噪声水平特征、损失加权和直方图分辨率作为联合可调的轴，统一放置在共享的LightGBM表面上进行配置搜索，将已发表的配置视为该空间的特殊情形。

Result: 在11个表格基准数据集上，采用fold-0调参、fold-1至5评估、匹配40次试验预算和采样器的设置下：(1) 选定的score-flex配置在每一个数据集上都优于发布基线（配对Wilcoxon 11/0，p<10^-3）；(2) 最佳聚合CRPS技能分数为0.725 vs 0.699；(3) score-flex配置用随机采样器换取更高精度，是较慢的一行；(4) 流匹配是最廉价的采样方案（比发布基线快5.2倍），且是校准最佳的DiffGBM行；(5) 调优后的非扩散基线仍在个别数据集上获胜；(6) 随机（epsilon>0）流采样器并未Pareto支配确定性角点。

Conclusion: 树基扩散模型的默认神经网络设计配置是性能的关键约束，将其显式化并联合调优可带来显著的精度提升。DiffGBM表明，流匹配路径提供了最快的采样速度和最佳校准度，而score-flex空间通过在共享LightGBM表面上联合调优各个配置维度全面超越发布基线。然而，即使在充分调优后，扩散生成方法也并非在全部数据集上一致超越传统非扩散基线，且随机流采样并不一定优于确定性采样。

Abstract: Tree-based diffusion models fit flexible conditional predictive distributions for tabular regression without a neural density estimator, but they inherit their design defaults---noising path, parameterization, training distribution, features, sampler---from the neural setting. We show these defaults are the binding constraint: what a gradient-boosted ensemble actually solves is a supervised regression problem whose conditioning they determine. We present DiffGBM, which makes them explicit along two axes. First, a Gaussian-path flow-matching trainer for $p(y \mid x)$ that learns a velocity field directly and recovers the score algebraically, admitting few-step deterministic ODE sampling. Second, we expose the score-side recipe---residualization, EDM-style preconditioning, log-sigma time sampling, noise-level features, loss weighting, and histogram resolution---as jointly tunable axes over a shared LightGBM surface rather than one frozen bundle. This \emph{score-flex} space represents the published recipe as a special case; across eleven tabular benchmarks under fold-0 tuning, folds-1--5 evaluation, and a matched 40-trial budget and sampler, the selected configurations beat that baseline on \emph{every} dataset (paired Wilcoxon $11/0$, $p<10^{-3}$), with the best aggregate CRPS skill (0.725 vs.\ 0.699) of any row. The two rows are complementary: score-flex buys accuracy with a stochastic sampler and is the slowest row, while flow matching is the cheapest sampler ($5.2\times$ faster than the published baseline) and the best-calibrated DiffGBM row. Tuned non-diffusion baselines still win individual datasets, and stochastic ($\varepsilon>0$) flow samplers do not Pareto-dominate the deterministic corner.

</details>


### [142] [Structured Neural Chaos: An Adaptive Surrogate Modeling Framework for Functional Uncertainty Quantification and Global Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28903)
*Isabel Corona Guevara,Yeping Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化神经混沌（sNC）展开作为基于方差的全剧灵敏度分析（GSA）的替代模型框架，融合了多项式混沌展开（PCE）的可解释性与正交结构，以及神经网络的表达能力，通过模仿截断的functional ANOVA分解并采用可分离低秩近似，可高效处理高维随机输入和功能响应的灵敏度分析问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于方差的全局灵敏度分析（GSA）在不确定性量化中至关重要，但重复模型评估通常极其昂贵。虽然多项式混沌展开（PCE）凭借其正交结构能与方差灵敏度度量直接关联，但它受限于维度灾难，且在处理功能响应时计算负担进一步放大。现有替代模型难以同时兼顾高维随机输入与功能响应场景下的可扩展性和可解释性。

Method: 提出Structured Neural Chaos（sNC）展开：它模仿截断的functional ANOVA分解，每个交互组分采用可分离低秩近似，基函数和系数均由神经网络参数化。展开采用顺序构建方式，自适应地识别每个ANOVA子空间内的主导模式并确定表示的有效复杂度。灵敏度统计量可直接从sNC展开系数中提取，计算成本可忽略不计。

Result: sNC框架保留了结构化分解的可解释性，同时利用神经网络的表达能力克服高维问题；其结构允许直接从展开系数中以极低成本提取统计量和灵敏度度量。

Conclusion: sNC扩展为高维随机输入和功能响应场景下的基于方差的GSA提供了一种兼具可扩展性与可解释性的有效替代建模框架，弥补了传统PCE在处理此类问题时面临的维度灾难挑战。

Abstract: Variance-based global sensitivity analysis (GSA) plays a key role in uncertainty quantification by identifying the contributions of uncertain inputs to the variability of the model response. The repeated model evaluations required for these tasks are often prohibitively expensive; surrogate models provide an efficient alternative by constructing inexpensive approximations of the underlying system response. Constructing surrogate models that combine scalability and interpretability for systems with high-dimensional stochastic inputs and functional responses remains challenging, particularly when sensitivity estimates are required across spatial or temporal domains. Polynomial chaos expansion (PCE) provides an effective framework for uncertainty propagation and sensitivity analysis due to its orthogonal structure and direct relationship with variance-based sensitivity measures. However, PCE suffers from the curse of dimensionality, whose computational burden is amplified for problems with functional responses. In this work, we introduce the Structured Neural Chaos (sNC) expansion as a surrogate modeling framework for variance-based GSA, inspired by the interpretability and orthogonal structure of PCE. The proposed framework retains the interpretability of structured decompositions while leveraging the expressive power of neural networks. The sNC expansion mirrors a truncated functional ANOVA decomposition, where each interaction component admits a separable low-rank approximation whose basis functions and coefficients are parameterized by neural networks. The expansion is constructed sequentially, adaptively identifying the dominant modes within each ANOVA subspace and determining the effective complexity of the representation. The resulting structure enables the extraction of statistical and sensitivity quantities directly from the coefficients of the sNC expansion at negligible cost.

</details>


### [143] [Persistent Convolution: A Topological Framework for AI Alignment Testing and Semantic Space Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29008)
*Tyler Ashoff,Jordan Rodu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑的多模态对齐测试方法，通过在模型的嵌入空间上执行形式化统计检验，来表征语义结构、概念分离和知识图谱对齐，从而提升不透明AI模型在部署、选择和比较过程中的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代不透明AI模型追求性能而牺牲可解释性，导致测试困难。模型开发者需要一种利用人工策划的知识结构进行对齐检验的模型比较技术。由于即使是相对简单的任务，其输入空间规模也很大，因此需要超越标准结果推理的对齐检查方法。

Method: 开发了一种基于拓扑的多模态对齐测试方法，在模型的嵌入空间上执行形式化统计检验，利用人工策划的知识结构来测试模型语义结构、概念分离和知识图谱的对齐程度。该方法还提供了与可能性理论的直观联系，并构建了从数据到部署的统一决策理论框架。

Result: 该方法已得到开发和演示，能够使不透明模型的部署、选择和比较过程更加可解释，同时为模型对齐提供了拓扑学意义上的形式化检验途径。

Conclusion: 基于拓扑的多模态对齐测试为解决不透明AI模型的可测试性和可解释性问题提供了一条可行路径，并通过可能性理论与决策理论的统一框架，为模型从数据到部署的整个过程提供了更系统的评估手段。

Abstract: Modern opaque AI models prize performance over interpretability, which makes testing difficult. However, formal statistical tests conducted on a model's embedding space can provide robust characterizations of semantic structure, concept separation, and knowledge graph alignment. Model developers would benefit from a model comparison technique that leverages human-curated knowledge structures to test alignment. The scale of the input space for even relatively simple tasks motivates the need for alignment checks that augment standard outcome reasoning. This work develops and demonstrates a topology-based multi-modal alignment test to make deployment, selection, and comparison of opaque models more interpretable. These methods also offer an intuitive connection to possibility theory and a unified decision theoretic framework from data to deployment.

</details>


### [144] [The Greedy Advantage in Finite-Horizon Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29375)
*Kai Zhou,Michael Lingzhi Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域下的多臂伯努利老虎机问题，提出了一类正则化贪心算法，首次推导出有限时域遗憾上界，并证明遗憾可分解为瞬态探索成本与随正则化强度指数衰减的次优收敛项，进而给出正则化参数的标定规则，以及在极限情形下对经典贪心策略的更紧遗憾界。实验表明标定的正则化贪心策略持续匹配或超越最先进算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂老虎机的经典算法注重渐近遗憾保证，但实际应用中往往面临有限且外部强加的决策时域。现有理论对有限时域情形下正则化贪心算法的性能缺乏理论刻画，因此需要为该场景开发具有有限时域遗憾保证的实用算法。

Method: 提出一类用于多臂伯努利老虎机的正则化贪心算法；推导有限时域遗憾包络（regret envelope），将遗憾分解为瞬态探索成本和随正则化强度指数衰减的次优收敛项；基于该分解推导正则化参数的标定规则，并以正则化强度趋于零作为极限情况获得经典贪心策略的更紧遗憾界。

Result: 首次给出正则化贪心老虎机的有限时域遗憾界；推导出正则化参数的原理性标定方法；在大量数值实验中，标定的正则化贪心策略与最先进算法相比持续持平或更优。

Conclusion: 正则化贪心策略可为有限时域老虎机问题提供有效且具有理论保证的解决方案，在实践场景中值得作为首选算法之一。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly rely on sequential experimentation to improve decision-making. While the multi-armed bandit literature has developed algorithms with strong asymptotic regret guarantees, many practical applications operate over finite and externally imposed horizons. Motivated by the finite-horizon setting, we develop a class of regularized greedy algorithms for multi-armed Bernoulli bandits. We derive the first finite-horizon regret envelopes for regularized greedy bandits, showing that finite-horizon regret decomposes into transient exploration costs and a suboptimal convergence term that decays exponentially with the regularization strength. This characterization yields principled calibration rules for the regularization parameters and, as a limiting case, sharper regret guarantees for the classical greedy policy. Across extensive numerical experiments, calibrated regularized greedy policies consistently match or outperform state-of-the-art algorithms. These results suggest that regularized greedy policies can provide an effective approach for finite-horizon bandit problems.

</details>


### [145] [Analytical and Bootstrap Confidence Intervals of Double Machine Learning: Simulation studies and an application to rural-urban difference in obesity prevalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29456)
*Haozheng Xu,Siyuan Ma,Qingyan Xiang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模模拟研究和真实数据分析，系统比较了双机器学习（DML）在不同机器学习算法选择下，解析置信区间与自助法置信区间的覆盖率表现，发现学习器选择对DML推断可靠性有重大影响，且样本量增大时覆盖率反而下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML是治疗效应估计中广泛使用的方法，允许灵活的机器学习方法估计干扰参数同时保持有效推断。但在实际应用中，研究者需要在众多机器学习算法中进行选择，而这一选择对DML方差估计的影响尚未得到充分刻画。

Method: 通过全面的模拟研究，比较DML解析置信区间与自助法置信区间在不同机器学习算法（OLS、LASSO、随机森林、LightGBM、神经网络）下的覆盖率表现，并在多种数据生成设置下评估偏差、置信区间宽度和覆盖率。此外，使用美国县域城乡差异的真实数据进一步验证。

Result: 结果显示解析置信区间与自助法置信区间的覆盖率表现存在显著差异，学习器选择对DML推断的可靠性起关键作用。意外发现，许多设置下样本量增大时，DML解析和自助法置信区间的覆盖率反而下降。真实数据分析进一步证实模型性能因学习器选择而不同，且更高的乡村化程度对县域肥胖患病率有统计学上显著的递增效应。

Conclusion: 学习器选择在DML推断中扮演关键角色，实践中不能忽视。样本量增大导致覆盖率下降的意外发现值得进一步理论探究。研究为应用研究者的学习器选择和置信区间构建方式提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Double Machine Learning (DML) is a popular approach for treatment effect estimation in various settings, which allows a wide range of flexible machine learning methods to be used for nuisance parameter estimation while preserving valid inference. In practice, however, applied researchers must choose among many machine learning algorithms for nuisance models, and the impact of this choice on the variance estimation of DML is not well characterized. We conduct a comprehensive simulation study to compare the coverage probability of DML confidence intervals across different machine learning algorithms. In this study, we compare (1) analytical confidence intervals derived by DML theory versus (2) bootstrap confidence interval. We use a set of learners including ordinary least squares, LASSO, Random Forest, LightGBM, and Neural Networks under different data generation settings. We evaluate the performance across difference settings by bias, confidence interval width, and most importantly, coverage probability. Our results show substantial variability in coverage performance across analytical and bootstrap confidence intervals, highlighting that learner choice plays a critical role in reliable DML inference. Surprisingly, we find that in many settings, when sample size increases, the coverage probability of both DML analytical and bootstrap confidence interval decreases. We further investigate coverage probabilities using a real dataset on rural urban differences among U.S. counties. The real data analysis discovers that (1) the model performance still varies by the learner choices and (2) greater rurality has a statistically significant increasing effect on county level obesity prevalence.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Seeing the Forest for the Trees: The Gaussian Process Limit of BART](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28844)
*Cory McCartan,Melody Huang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝叶斯加性回归树（BART）在树数量趋于无穷时的极限行为，证明其收敛于一个具有特定核函数的高斯过程（GP），并提出了"随机树特征"（random tree features）作为该极限高斯过程的近似，证明了在这些随机特征上做岭回归可以达到极小极大最优学习率，且对维度的依赖仅为对数级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BART在预测和因果推断中表现出色，但以往的理论工作通过建立后验收缩率来解释其性能时，这些收缩率对协变量数量有强烈依赖。本文采用不同的理论视角——研究树数量趋于无穷时BART的极限行为，以期更深入地解释BART优越性能背后的原因。

Method: 1) 证明当树数量趋于无穷时，BART收敛于具有特定核函数的高斯过程；2) 分析该核函数及对应再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）的良好推断性质；3) 引入"随机树特征"作为极限高斯过程的近似；4) 建立在这些随机特征上做岭回归的极小极大最优学习率，该速率对维度仅具有对数依赖。

Result: 证明了BART在树数量趋于无穷时收敛于特定核的高斯过程；该核及RKHS具有良好的推断性质；随机树特征上的岭回归达到极小极大最优学习率，维度依赖仅为对数级别；随机树特征相比传统MCMC估计具有计算优势，且能轻松融入任何具有线性预测器的模型。

Conclusion: 本文从极限理论视角解释了BART的经验成功，同时随机树特征不仅提供了理论洞见，还带来了计算上的效率提升，并扩展了BART的适用范围和灵活性，使其可以方便地嵌入到任何具有线性预测器的模型中。

Abstract: Bayesian Additive Regression Trees (BART) have shown state-of-the-art performance in both prediction and causal inference problems. Previous theoretical work has attempted to explain BART's superior performance by establishing posterior contraction rates for standard BART models, but these rates depend strongly on the number of covariates. Here, we take a different approach and study the behavior of BART as the number of trees grows towards infinity. We show that in this regime, BART converges to a Gaussian process (GP) with a particular kernel. The kernel and its corresponding reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) have favorable inferential properties that help explain BART's excellent performance. We introduce *random tree features* as an approximation to this limiting GP, and establish minimax-optimal learning rates for ridge regression on these random features that depend only logarithmically on dimension. In addition to providing insight into the empirical success of BART, random tree features offer a computational benefit over traditional MCMC estimation. The random-features approximation also allows practitioners to easily incorporate BART into any model which has a linear predictor, expanding the applicability and flexibility of BART.

</details>


### [147] [A Simple Example of Bayesian Nonparametric Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28958)
*Antonio R. Linero*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个简单的反例，展示了对分布函数具有全支撑先验的贝叶斯模型也可能产生不一致的后验。具体地，在Dirichlet过程混合模型中对浓度参数α赋予重尾（指数或更重）先验时会导致后验不一致，而轻尾先验则能保证一致性，统一并推广了Freedman-Diaconis(1983)的一致性结果和Ferguson等人(1992)的不一致性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望通过一个极其简单的例子来揭示一个重要的理论现象：即使先验对分布函数具有全支撑（full support），贝叶斯后验仍可能不一致。这一反例具有教学价值，能够帮助研究者深入理解贝叶斯非参数方法的性质，并统一此前看似矛盾的文献结果。

Method: 考虑贝叶斯分层模型Y_i~P0，采用混合Dirichlet过程先验P~∫DP(α,H)πα(α)dα。通过对浓度参数α的边际先验πα赋予不同尾部分布（指数/重尾 vs 轻尾），分析相应的后验一致性性质，从而证明重尾先验导致不一致、轻尾先验保证一致性。

Result: 当πα(·)具有指数（或更重）的尾部时，即使该混合先验对分布函数具有全支撑，后验仍是不一致的；而当πα(·)为轻尾先验时，获得了后验一致性。该结果统一了Freedman和Diaconis(1983)的一致性结果与Ferguson等人(1992)描述的不一致性结果。

Conclusion: 全支撑先验并不足以保证贝叶斯后验的一致性，浓度参数的先验尾部行为对后验一致性起关键作用。该简洁例证说明了对非参数先验的超参数选择需要谨慎，并弥合了文献中一致性与不一致性结果之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: I present a very simple example in which a full-support prior over distribution functions has an inconsistent posterior, which I believe has instructive value. The example is $Y_i \stackrel{\text{iid}}{\sim} P_0$ under the Bayesian hierarchical model $[Y_i \mid P] \stackrel{\text{iid}}{\sim} P$ and $P \sim \int \mbox{DP}(α, H) \, π_α(α) \ dα$ when $π_α(\cdot)$ has exponential (or heavier) tails, where $\mbox{DP}(α, H)$ denotes a Dirichlet process with concentration parameter $α$ and mean $H(\cdot)$; on the other hand, consistency is obtained under a light-tailed prior. This unifies and generalizes a consistency result of Freedman and Diaconis (1983) with an inconsistency result described by Ferguson et al. (1992).

</details>


### [148] [Asymptotic Efficiency of the Global Maximum-Likelihood Estimator in Multi-Emitter Localization Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28985)
*Yi Sun*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文通过期望最大化算法实现GML估计器，并在接近真实位置的初始值附近初始化，通过仿真验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该摘要未明确说明研究动机，但从内容可推断其旨在实现和验证GML估计器在实际应用中的可行性。

Method: 采用期望最大化算法来求解GML估计器，并在接近真实位置的初始值处进行初始化，随后通过仿真实验进行验证。

Result: 通过仿真验证了理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的EM算法实现的GML估计器能够在接近真实位置初始化时有效工作，且仿真结果支持理论分析。

Abstract: We realize the GML estimator by an expectation-maximization algorithm initialized near the true positions and verify the theory by simulation.

</details>


### [149] [Möbius transport on spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29280)
*Eduargo García-Portugués,Shogo Kato*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文揭示球面Cauchy分布可通过Möbius变换从均匀分布生成，其本质是余弦关于位置轴单调重排的一维最优传输的切法提升。由此提出推广：将Cauchy目标替换为任意旋转对称分布（如Poisson核或球面cardioid），可得到广义Möbius变换。以von Mises-Fisher为基底的Möbius传输可生成具有闭式密度、保留归一化常数且便于模拟的各向异性球面分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Möbius变换的概率本质，并在此基础上设计更一般、更灵活的各向异性球面分布族，以克服经典分布（如von Mises-Fisher）在各向异性建模上的局限性。

Method: 将生成球面Cauchy分布的Möbius变换识别为沿位置轴的余弦单调重排这一最优传输的切法提升；通过将Cauchy目标分布推广为任意旋转对称分布（如Poisson核、球面cardioid），构造广义Möbius变换；将von Mises-Fisher基底与Möbius传输结合，得到密度具有闭式表达式且保留基底归一化常数的各向异性分布，并支持直接模拟。

Result: 提出了Möbius-von Mises-Fisher分布及各向同性缩放von Mises-Fisher分布（后者亦由Möbius传输产生），并将其应用于古地磁方向和短周期彗星轨道数据，结果表明该方法优于经典分布及近期提出的替代方案。

Conclusion: Möbius变换的传输解读为球面分布构造提供了统一的理论框架，推广后的广义Möbius变换能够生成兼具解析可处理性与建模灵活性的各向异性球面分布，在实际数据中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: The Möbius transformation that generates the spherical Cauchy distribution from the uniform is the tangent-normal lift of a one-dimensional optimal transport: the monotone rearrangement of the cosine about the location axis. This identifies the probabilistic nature of the Möbius transformation and suggests a generalization: replacing the Cauchy target by any rotationally symmetric law, for instance the Poisson kernel or spherical cardioid, yields a generalized Möbius transformation. Möbius transport of a von Mises-Fisher base gives tractable anisotropic distributions on the sphere, with closed-form densities that inherit the base normalizing constant and allow immediate simulation. The Möbius-von Mises-Fisher and isotropic scaled von Mises-Fisher distributions, the latter also arising from a Möbius transport, are illustrated on paleomagnetic directions and short-period comet orbits, where they outperform classical and recently proposed alternatives.

</details>


### [150] [Asymptotic Minimax Estimation under Global-Local Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29498)
*Rhitankar Bandyopadhyay,Malay Ghosh*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了全局-局部收缩先验在高维稀疏数据中的理论性质，突破了以往文献中假设样本方差已知的限制，将误差方差也赋予先验分布，并开发了收缩因子新的尾部界，进而推导出后验均值的渐近极小极大收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局-局部收缩先验是高维稀疏数据分析的重要工具，但现有理论保证均假设样本方差已知，这一限制不符合实际应用场景。本文旨在放宽该假设，为误差方差赋予先验，从而提供更普适的理论保障。

Method: 在原有全局-局部先验框架基础上，对误差方差也赋予先验分布，放宽了样本方差已知的假设；开发了收缩因子（shrinkage factors）的新尾部界（tail bounds），并利用这些结果推导后验均值的渐近极小极大收敛速率。

Result: 成功放宽了样本方差已知的假设，在误差方差也赋予先验的情况下，获得了收缩因子新的尾部界，并据此建立了感兴趣参数后验均值的渐近极小极大速率。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了全局-局部收缩先验的理论覆盖范围，使其在更一般（更符合实际）的设定下仍具备渐近最优性保证，填补了现有文献中关于误差方差未知情形下理论保障的空白。

Abstract: Global-local priors, often also referred to as shrinkage priors, have proved to be a very effective tool for the analysis of high dimensional data under sparsity. Asymptotic theoretical properties of such priors, studied under various scenarios, are now available in the literature. However, to our knowledge, theoretical guarantees of such priors provided so far, involve the assumption of known sample variance. The present paper relaxes this assumption, and carries out the analysis with a prior assigned to the error variance as well. In the process, some new tail bounds for shrinkage factors are developed, and these results are then utilized in providing asymptotic minimax rates for the posterior means of the parameters of interest.

</details>


### [151] [Adversarially robust multiple testing in high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29507)
*Anders Bredahl Kock,David Preinerstorfer*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出基于分位数缩尾（quantile-winsorization）技术的稳健多重检验方法，用于评估高维均值向量坐标上的等式约束，在对抗性污染下近似强控制家族误差率（FWER），并允许假设数量随样本量指数增长，仅需略多于二阶矩的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维环境中检验均值向量坐标的等式约束时，传统多重检验方法对数据污染（outlier/contamination）敏感，且高维假设检验面临维度诅咒。现有方法通常需要较强的矩条件或无法应对对抗性污染，因此需要开发既能稳健对抗污染又能在超高维场景下控制FWER的检验程序。

Method: 该程序基于分位数缩尾（quantile-winsorization）技术对数据进行稳健化处理。单样本结果依赖高维分位数缩尾均值分布的高斯逼近不等式；两样本结果则基于作者在本文中扩展的高斯逼近不等式，这些扩展结果本身具有独立的理论价值。

Result: 所提方法在对抗性污染下能够近似强控制FWER，且允许假设数量随样本量指数增长（即维数可以远大于样本量），同时仅需样本存在略多于二阶矩的矩条件即可成立。

Conclusion: 该研究为高维均值向量坐标等式约束的稳健多重检验提供了可行方案，通过分位数缩尾与高斯逼近的技术结合，兼顾了对抗污染的鲁棒性、高维可扩展性以及宽松的矩条件要求。两样本高斯逼近不等式的扩展也为后续研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Robust multiple testing procedures for assessing equality restrictions on the coordinates of high-dimensional mean vectors are proposed. Our procedures are based on quantile-winsorization techniques, approximately control the familywise error rate (strongly) under adversarial contamination, and allow the number of hypotheses to grow exponentially with sample size, despite requiring only slightly more than two moments. Technically, our one-sample results build on recent Gaussian approximation inequalities for the distribution of high-dimensional quantile-winsorized means, whereas our two-sample results are based on extensions thereof, which we develop here and which could be of some interest in their own right.

</details>


### [152] [Joint parameters estimation in cubic tensor model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29619)
*Sumit Mukherjee,Arnab Sen,Qiang Wu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于三次张量相互作用的吉布斯测度中，从单一观测中联合估计参数的问题，重点关注最大伪似然估计量，并给出了联合一致性和渐近病态性的可检验条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究由稠密指数随机图模型（ERGMs）、算术级数模型和非齐次随机超图等应用驱动，旨在解决高维三次张量吉布斯测度中从单次观测进行联合参数估计的挑战。

Method: 聚焦于最大伪似然估计量，开发了三次张量吉布斯测度的非线性大偏差和平均场近似工具，用于推导联合一致性和渐近病态性的可检验条件。

Result: 对于边-三角形ERGM，伪似然在铁磁区（非负场）下病态，但在足够强的反铁磁区下一致；对于边-三叉星ERGM，在所有逆温度和外部场下均病态；此外还研究了算术级数和非齐次超图模型的一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的三次张量吉布斯测度的分析工具具有广泛的应用前景，不仅限于文中讨论的具体模型。

Abstract: We study joint parameter estimation from a single observation in high-dimensional Gibbs measures with cubic tensor interactions, motivated by dense ERGMs, arithmetic-progression models, and inhomogeneous random hypergraphs. Focusing on the maximum pseudolikelihood estimator, we give checkable conditions for joint consistency and asymptotic ill-conditioning. For the edge-triangle ERGM, pseudolikelihood is ill-conditioned in the ferromagnetic regime with nonnegative field, but consistent in a sufficiently strong antiferromagnetic regime. For the edge-three-star ERGM, it is ill-conditioned for all inverse temperatures and external fields. We also study consistency for arithmetic-progression, and inhomogeneous hypergraph models. Our proofs develop nonlinear large-deviation and mean-field approximation tools for cubic tensor Gibbs measures, which have scope for broad applications.

</details>


### [153] [Differentially Private Nonparametric Modal Learning with Applications to Regression and Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29675)
*Arkajyoti Bhattacharjee,Arnab Auddy*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-GRAMS，一种在严格差分隐私约束下恢复多元密度模态的方法。该方法基于均值漂移思想，对隐私保护的评分估计器执行带噪梯度上升，结合偏置降低的高阶核、梯度裁剪和高斯噪声校准，并提供隐私初始化方案与相关噪声的联合发布机制。理论证明可以高概率恢复所有总体模态，给出渐近误差率并建立极小极大下界；还提出两个扩展：DP-PMS（隐私模态回归）和DP-GRAMS-C（聚类流水线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度模态为多模态分布提供局部化和可解释的摘要，但在严格差分隐私约束下的模态估计尚未充分探索。现有隐私密度估计工作通常关注密度本身而非其模态结构，而模态恢复对误差敏感且初始化困难，因此需要专门设计隐私友好的算法。

Method: DP-GRAMS采用均值漂移范式：1) 使用偏置降低的高阶核构建隐私评分估计器（对Hölder平滑参数β>2的局部密度）；2) 在梯度上升步骤中通过梯度裁剪和校准高斯噪声施加隐私；3) 隐私初始化方案结合密度感知效用函数和抑制规则，在公共网格上采样k个起点，逐步抑制已选局部邻域以覆盖模态盆地；4) 多个起点的相关性噪声实现单一(ε,δ)-差分隐私保证下的联合发布。

Result: 理论结果表明所有总体模态均以高概率恢复，渐近误差率为O(((log n)/n)^{2(β-1)/(d+2β)}) + O(((polylog(n,δ))/(n²ε²))^{(β-1)/(d+β)})。提供隐私模态估计的极小极大下界，证明估计器在MSE意义上接近最优（仅差对数因子）。在合成和真实数据上的广泛实验表明相对于常见基线具有更优的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 本文填补了隐私密度模态估计的理论空白，给出了在严格差分隐私下精准恢复多元密度模态的方法、理论保证和极小极大最优性，并展示了方法的可扩展性（模态回归与聚类）。该工作为隐私保护的分布结构性推断建立了新的基准。

Abstract: Density modes provide a localized and interpretable summary of multimodal distributions, but their estimation under rigorous differential privacy constraints remains largely unexplored. We study differentially private recovery of density modes for multivariate distributions under local smoothness, curvature, and separation conditions. We propose DP-GRAMS, a mean-shift inspired method that performs noisy ascent on a differentially private score estimator. Assuming the density belongs locally to a Hölder class with smoothness parameter $β> 2$, our score estimator uses bias-reducing higher-order kernels, and then enforces privacy in the gradient ascent steps via gradient clipping and calibrated Gaussian noise. A private initialization scheme combines a density-aware utility with a suppression rule and, with $k\asymp M\log n$ draws over a public $h_{\mathrm{DAP}}$-grid and suppression radius $ρ_{\mathrm{init}}\asymp (\log n)^{-1/d}$, achieves high-probability coverage of the modal basins by successively suppressing selected local neighborhoods in competitive regions, while correlated noise across multiple starts enables joint release under a single $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy guarantee. We prove that all population modes are recovered with high probability and establish asymptotic error rates of the form $O\!\left((\tfrac{\log n}{n})^{\frac{2(β-1)}{d+2β}}\right) + O\!\left((\tfrac{\mathrm{polylog}(n,δ)}{n^2\varepsilon^2})^{\frac{β-1}{d+β}}\right)$. We also provide minimax lower bounds for private mode estimation, and show that our estimators are nearly optimal, up to a logarithmic factor in the MSE. We present two natural extensions: DP-PMS, a private modal-regression method, and DP-GRAMS-C, a clustering pipeline. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real data demonstrate favorable privacy-utility trade-offs relative to common baselines.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Topology-Aware Data Movement for Disaggregated GPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28633)
*Sanjeev Rao Ganjihal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个拓扑感知的KV缓存传输编排器，解决LLM推理分解化（prefill与decode分离）后KV缓存跨GPU池传输的网络瓶颈问题。通过感知互连层级（NVLink/InfiniBand/TCP带宽差异高达72倍）选择最优传输路径，结合流水线分层传输、MoE专家调度与KV缓存局部性联合优化、以及CXL 3.0内存扩展器作为溢出层，实现在无完整真实硬件下投影分析显示3到18倍传输延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分解式LLM推理系统（如DistServe、Splitwise、Mooncake）都采用统一的RDMA传输，完全忽视了GPU间物理关系导致的巨大带宽差异：NVLink域内900 GB/s、跨节点InfiniBand 50 GB/s、跨数据中心TCP仅12.5 GB/s，差距达72倍。70B模型每次请求需传输2.6 GB KV缓存，生产规模下超100 GB/s的聚合需求，若不做拓扑感知的传输调度，将严重限制推理性能。

Method: 设计了一套拓扑感知的传输编排器，包含三个核心技术：1) 流水线化分层传输，将KV缓存按层pipeline传输以与prefill计算重叠，隐藏60-85%的延迟；2) 对MoE模型引入NVLink域感知的放置策略，联合优化专家分发与KV缓存局部性；3) 利用CXL 3.0内存扩展器作为共享溢出层，提供6倍容量且延迟比NVMe低86倍。系统在启动时自动发现互连层级并根据每次传输选择最优传输路径。

Result: 由于完整评估需要多节点异构互连集群和CXL 3.0硬件（超出学术资源且GPU云尚不可用），论文提供了解析带宽模型、组件实现以及三种架构下的投影分析，结果显示相对于统一RDMA方案可实现3到18倍的传输延迟降低。

Conclusion: 分解式LLM推理的KV缓存传输必须感知底层互连拓扑，而非简单使用统一RDMA。拓扑感知编排结合分层pipeline、MoE感知放置与CXL 3.0新型内存层，能够从根本上解决KV缓存传输瓶颈，为下一代分解式推理基础设施提供重要的设计方向。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM inference creates a datacenter networking problem that no existing system solves correctly. When prefill and decode run on separate GPU pools, the KV cache must be transferred between them. For a 70B model this is 2.6 GB per request, exceeding 100 GB/s aggregate at production scale. Yet DistServe, Splitwise, and Mooncake all use uniform RDMA, ignoring that bandwidth between two GPUs varies by 72x depending on their physical relationship: 900 GB/s via NVLink within a domain, 50 GB/s via InfiniBand across nodes, 12.5 GB/s via TCP across data centers. We design a topology-aware transfer orchestrator that discovers interconnect hierarchy at startup and selects optimal transport per transfer. Three mechanisms work together: (1) pipelined layer-by-layer transfer that overlaps transmission with ongoing prefill, hiding 60 to 85 percent of latency behind computation; (2) NVLink domain-aware placement for Mixture-of-Experts models that co-optimizes expert dispatch with KV cache locality; and (3) CXL 3.0 memory expanders as a shared overflow tier providing 6x capacity at 86x lower latency than NVMe. Full evaluation requires multi-node clusters with heterogeneous interconnects and CXL 3.0 hardware that is beyond academic resources and not yet available in GPU clouds. We present analytical bandwidth models, component implementations, and projected analysis across three architectures showing 3 to 18x transfer latency reduction over uniform RDMA.

</details>


### [155] [Sensitivity Analysis of GRU, LSTM and Transformer Encoder in Classification of Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28665)
*Bidhya Shrestha,Christos Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer编码器）通过车辆遥测数据识别Level 2自动驾驶系统（Comma Openpilot、Tesla Autopilot、Cadillac Super Cruise和手动驾驶）的能力，并引入了一个模块化鲁棒性评估框架来模拟五类遥测数据退化（连续通道噪声、突发丢失、延迟转换、虚假切换和跨特征不一致），发现时间抖动是导致模型性能崩溃（macro F1降至0.44-0.50）的主要脆弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来的软件定义汽车（SDVs）可能同时运行多个原生和后装自动驾驶系统（如Comma.ai的Openpilot），独立监控系统以验证哪个自动驾驶系统正在运行对于安全监控、法规合规、保险评估和异常检测至关重要。当前缺乏对基于遥测数据的ADS识别模型的系统性鲁棒性评估。

Method: 首先训练三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer编码器）在干净遥测数据上识别四种驾驶模式（三种ADS+手动驾驶）；然后提出模块化鲁棒性评估框架，模拟五类遥测退化（AWGN加累积漂移、相关跨通道噪声、时间抖动、突发丢失、延迟转换、虚假切换、跨特征不一致），在五级严重度（L1-L5）下用macro-F1评估模型鲁棒性。

Result: 干净数据上三种模型macro F1达到0.90-0.93；威胁匹配训练得到0.904-0.916的macro F1且仅适度损失干净数据性能。事件级损坏对性能影响较小（L5时仍≥0.87），但时间抖动使所有模型的macro-F1崩溃至0.44-0.50。

Conclusion: 序列模型在干净数据上能有效区分Level 2自动驾驶系统，但时间抖动是主要的鲁棒性弱点，需要在部署前针对性增强模型对时间对齐扰动的抵抗能力。

Abstract: Automated driving systems (ADSs) are becoming ubiquitous. Future Software Defined Vehicles (SDVs) may be able to run multiple ADSs, both native and aftermarket such as Comma.ai's Openpilot. Monitoring systems to independently verify which automated driving system is active are important for safety monitoring, regulatory compliance, insurance assessment, and anomaly detection. In this paper, we first evaluate the effectiveness of three sequence-based classification models: Gated Recurrent Units (GRU), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and a Transformer encoder model for identifying Level 2 automated driving systems using vehicle telematics data alone: Comma Openpilot, Tesla Autopilot, and Cadillac Super Cruise, along with manual driving. All three models achieve strong clean-data performance with macro F1-scores of 0.92 (GRU), 0.90 (LSTM), and 0.93 (Transformer encoder model) when trained on clean data; threat-matched training yields 0.904-0.916 macro F1 with only a modest clean-data penalty. Second, we introduce a modular robustness evaluation framework that simulates realistic telematics degradation through five corruption families at five severity levels (L1-L5). Continuous channels are perturbed using additive white Gaussian noise with cumulative drift, correlated cross-channel noise, and temporal jitter. Binary event signals are subjected to burst loss, delayed transitions, spurious toggles and cross-feature inconsistencies inspired by communication errors. Robustness is measured using macro-F1, which gives equal weight to each class and is suitable for imbalanced multiclass evaluation. Our evaluation reveals a sharp failure-mode split: event-level corruptions reduce macro-F1 only slightly (greater than equal to 0.87 at L5), while temporal jitter collapses macro-F1 to 0.44-0.50 across GRU, LSTM, and Transformer encoder model.

</details>


### [156] [Guarantees on Dynamical System Distinguishability for LLM Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28667)
*Mohamed Akrout,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了将LLM响应分类建模为黑盒动力系统（DS）轨迹的方法，通过二元假设检验框架证明：忽略动力学的分类器存在基础准确率下限，而DS分类器的误分类概率随序列长度L指数衰减，并建立了跨嵌入泛化的理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于动力学的LLM响应分类方法在经验上取得了成功，但其理论基础严重缺乏，包括：为什么该方法有效、如何随token序列长度扩展、以及何时能跨嵌入模型迁移。需要严格的数学理论来解释这些经验现象。

Method: 将分类任务形式化为两个随机线性动力系统之间的二元假设检验问题。具体包括：（1）分析两个DS平稳边际分布之间的全变差距离，（2）推导DS分类器误分类概率的指数衰减率及其与动力学可区分性量δ²的关系，（3）引入近似交织条件来刻画跨嵌入泛化，并用交织映射的最小奇异值建立可迁移可区分性的下界。

Result: （1）证明即使两个DS的动力学差异显著，其平稳边际分布的全变差距离可以任意小，这为忽略token动力学的分类器提供了基础准确率下限；（2）证明DS分类的误分类概率随序列长度L指数衰减，衰减率由捕获两个DS谱距离的可区分性量δ²决定；（3）通过近似交织条件建立了跨嵌入可迁移可区分性的下界。

Conclusion: 这些理论结果解释了DS分类方法在经验上的成功，并为利用DS理论分析AI系统（而非用AI建模动力系统的常规路径）提供了进一步研究的动机。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that classifying large language models (LLMs)' responses can be distinguished by modeling token embeddings as trajectories of a black-box dynamical system (DS) and comparing prediction residuals of two DSs. Despite the empirical success of this dynamical approach, a theoretical understanding of why it works, how well it scales as a function of the token sequence, and when it transfers across embedding models remains lacking.
